Load the data\n    strain = types.load_timeseries(data_path, channel_name)\n\n    # Print some basic info\n    print(f\"Data duration: {strain.duration}\")\n    print(f\"Sampling rate: {strain.sample_rate} Hz\")\n    print(f\"Data start time: {strain.start_time}\")\n\n    # Remove low frequency noise with high-pass filter (typically at 30 Hz for LIGO)\n    strain = highpass_fir(strain, 30, remove_corrupted=True)\n\n    # Resample to consistent rate (typically 2048 Hz for analysis)\n    strain = resample_to_rate(strain, 2048)\n\n    # Estimate PSD for noise characterization\n    psd = strain.psd(4)  # Use 4 seconds for PSD estimation\n\n    # Interpolate PSD to match strain data length\n    psd = interpolate(psd, strain.delta_f)\n\n    # Truncate inverse PSD to avoid time domain wraparound\n    psd = inverse_spectrum_truncation(psd, int(4 * strain.sample_rate),\n                                      low_frequency_cutoff=30.0,\n                                      domain=types.FREQ)\n\n    print(\"Data conditioning complete.\")\n    return strain, psd\n\ndef highpass_fir(strain, frequency, remove_corrupted=True):\n    \"\"\"Apply high-pass FIR filter.\"\"\"\n    from pycbc.filter import highpass_fir as hp_fir\n    return hp_fir(strain, frequency, remove_corrupted=remove_corrupted)\n\ndef resample_to_rate(strain, rate):\n    \"\"\"Resample data to specified rate.\"\"\"\n    if strain.sample_rate != rate:\n        return resample_poly(strain, rate, order=8)\n    return strain\n\ndef resample_to_rate(strain, rate):\n    \"\"\"Resample data to specified rate.\"\"\"\n    from pycbc.filter import resample_poly\n    if strain.sample_rate != rate:\n        return resample_poly(strain, rate, order=8)\n    return strain\n\ndef create_template_bank(mass_range, approximant, strain):\n    \"\"\"\n    Create a template bank of waveforms over mass range.\n\n    Args:\n        mass_range: Tuple of (min_mass, max_mass)\n        approximant: Waveform approximant name\n        strain: Reference strain data for timing\n\n    Returns:\n        List of template waveforms\n    \"\"\"\n    min_mass, max_mass = mass_range\n    templates = []\n\n    print(f\"Creating template bank for {approximant}...\")\n\n    # Generate templates for all mass combinations (mass1, mass2)\n    for mass1 in range(min_mass, max_mass + 1):\n        for mass2 in range(min_mass, max_mass + 1):\n            # Generate waveform template\n            try:\n                hp, _ = get_td_waveform(\n                    approximant=approximant,\n                    mass1=mass1,\n                    mass2=mass2,\n                    f_lower=30,  # Start frequency\n                    delta_t=strain.delta_t,\n                    distance=500  # Distance in Mpc (arbitrary for detection)\n                )\n\n                # Resize template to match strain length\n                template = hp\n                templates.append({\n                    'template': template,\n                    'mass1': mass1,\n                    'mass2': mass2,\n                    'total_mass': mass1 + mass2\n                })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Warning: Could not generate template for mass1={mass1}, mass2={mass2}: {e}\")\n\n    print(f\"Created {len(templates)} templates for {approximant}\")\n    return templates\n\ndef perform_matched_filtering(strain, psd, templates, approximant):\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform matched filtering to find the best matching template.\n\n    Args:\n        strain: Conditioned strain data\n        psd: Power spectral density\n        templates: List of template waveforms\n        approximant: Name of the approximant\n\n    Returns:\n        Best match (template info and SNR)\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Performing matched filtering for {approximant}...\")\n\n    best_snr = -1\n    best_template = None\n    best_template_info = None\n\n    for template_info in templates:\n        template = template_info['template']\n\n        # Align template with strain data\n        template = template.duplicate()\n        template = template.crop(2, 1.5)  # Remove corrupted samples\n\n        # Perform matched filtering\n        try:\n            snr = matched_filter(template, strain, psd=psd, low_frequency_cutoff=30)\n\n            # Find peak SNR\n            snr = snr.crop(4, 4)  # Remove edge effects\n            peak_snr = abs(snr).max().item()\n\n            if peak_snr > best_snr:\n                best_snr = peak_snr\n                best_template = template\n                best_template_info = template_info\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Filtering failed for mass1={template_info['mass1']}, mass2={template_info['mass2']}: {e}\")\n\n    if best_template_info is not None:\n        print(f\"Best match for {approximant}: SNR={best_snr:.2f}, Total Mass={best_template_info['total_mass']} Msun\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"No valid matches found for {approximant}\")\n\n    return {\n        'approximant': approximant,\n        'snr': best_snr,\n        'total_mass': best_template_info['total_mass'] if best_template_info else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main analysis function.\"\"\"\n    # Configuration\n    data_path = '/root/data/PyCBC_T2_2.gwf'\n    channel_name = 'H1:TEST-STRAIN'\n    mass_range = (10, 40)\n    approximants = ['SEOBNRv4_opt', 'IMRPhenomD', 'TaylorT4']\n    output_path = '/root/detection_results.csv'\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Gravitational Wave Detection Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"PyCBC version: {pycbc.__version__}\")\n\n    # Load and condition data\n    strain, psd = load_and_condition_data(data_path, channel_name)\n\n    # Process each approximant\n    results = []\n\n    for approximant in approximants:\n        print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n        print(f\"Processing approximant: {approximant}\")\n        print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n        # Create template bank\n        templates = create_template_bank(mass_range, approximant, strain)\n\n        # Perform matched filtering\n        result = perform_matched_filtering(strain, psd, templates, approximant)\n        results.append(result)\n\n    # Write results to CSV\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Writing results to CSV...\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    df = pd.DataFrame(results)\n    df = df[['approximant', 'snr', 'total_mass']]  # Ensure correct column order\n    df.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n\n    print(f\"\\nResults saved to {output_path}\")\n    print(\"\\nFinal Results:\")\n    print(df.to_string(index=False))\n\n    return results\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"}}],"model":"MiniMax-M2","stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":149,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":419,"cache_read_input_tokens":17407},"service_tier":"standard","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9b70bbec-a033-4e0d-9e8a-442c904d8bf5","uuid":"85865a6b-5bf1-4e51-9bd3-9e37bb06f726"}

Truncate inverse PSD to avoid time domain wraparound\n    psd = inverse_spectrum_truncation(psd, int(4 * strain.sample_rate),\n                                      low_frequency_cutoff=30.0,\n                                      domain=types.FREQ)\n\n    print(\"Data conditioning complete.\")\n    return strain, psd\n\ndef highpass_fir(strain, frequency, remove_corrupted=True):\n    \"\"\"Apply high-pass FIR filter.\"\"\"\n    from pycbc.filter import highpass_fir as hp_fir\n    return hp_fir(strain, frequency, remove_corrupted=remove_corrupted)\n\ndef resample_to_rate(strain, rate):\n    \"\"\"Resample data to specified rate.\"\"\"\n    if strain.sample_rate != rate:\n        return resample_poly(strain, rate, order=8)\n    return strain\n\ndef resample_to_rate(strain, rate):\n    \"\"\"Resample data to specified rate.\"\"\"\n    from pycbc.filter import resample_poly\n    if strain.sample_rate != rate:\n        return resample_poly(strain, rate, order=8)\n    return strain\n\ndef create_template_bank(mass_range, approximant, strain):\n    \"\"\"\n    Create a template bank of waveforms over mass range.\n\n    Args:\n        mass_range: Tuple of (min_mass, max_mass)\n        approximant: Waveform approximant name\n        strain: Reference strain data for timing\n\n    Returns:\n        List of template waveforms\n    \"\"\"\n    min_mass, max_mass = mass_range\n    templates = []\n\n    print(f\"Creating template bank for {approximant}...\")\n\n    # Generate templates for all mass combinations (mass1, mass2)\n    for mass1 in range(min_mass, max_mass + 1):\n        for mass2 in range(min_mass, max_mass + 1):\n            # Generate waveform template\n            try:\n                hp, _ = get_td_waveform(\n                    approximant=approximant,\n                    mass1=mass1,\n                    mass2=mass2,\n                    f_lower=30,  # Start frequency\n                    delta_t=strain.delta_t,\n                    distance=500  # Distance in Mpc (arbitrary for detection)\n                )\n\n                # Resize template to match strain length\n                template = hp\n                templates.append({\n                    'template': template,\n                    'mass1': mass1,\n                    'mass2': mass2,\n                    'total_mass': mass1 + mass2\n                })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Warning: Could not generate template for mass1={mass1}, mass2={mass2}: {e}\")\n\n    print(f\"Created {len(templates)} templates for {approximant}\")\n    return templates\n\ndef perform_matched_filtering(strain, psd, templates, approximant):\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform matched filtering to find the best matching template.\n\n    Args:\n        strain: Conditioned strain data\n        psd: Power spectral density\n        templates: List of template waveforms\n        approximant: Name of the approximant\n\n    Returns:\n        Best match (template info and SNR)\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Performing matched filtering for {approximant}...\")\n\n    best_snr = -1\n    best_template = None\n    best_template_info = None\n\n    for template_info in templates:\n        template = template_info['template']\n\n        # Align template with strain data\n        template = template.duplicate()\n        template = template.crop(2, 1.5)  # Remove corrupted samples\n\n        # Perform matched filtering\n        try:\n            snr = matched_filter(template, strain, psd=psd, low_frequency_cutoff=30)\n\n            # Find peak SNR\n            snr = snr.crop(4, 4)  # Remove edge effects\n            peak_snr = abs(snr).max().item()\n\n            if peak_snr > best_snr:\n                best_snr = peak_snr\n                best_template = template\n                best_template_info = template_info\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Filtering failed for mass1={template_info['mass1']}, mass2={template_info['mass2']}: {e}\")\n\n    if best_template_info is not None:\n        print(f\"Best match for {approximant}: SNR={best_snr:.2f}, Total Mass={best_template_info['total_mass']} Msun\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"No valid matches found for {approximant}\")\n\n    return {\n        'approximant': approximant,\n        'snr': best_snr,\n        'total_mass': best_template_info['total_mass'] if best_template_info else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main analysis function.\"\"\"\n    # Configuration\n    data_path = '/root/data/PyCBC_T2_2.gwf'\n    channel_name = 'H1:TEST-STRAIN'\n    mass_range = (10, 40)\n    approximants = ['SEOBNRv4_opt', 'IMRPhenomD', 'TaylorT4']\n    output_path = '/root/detection_results.csv'\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Gravitational Wave Detection Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"PyCBC version: {pycbc.__version__}\")\n\n    # Load and condition data\n    strain, psd = load_and_condition_data(data_path, channel_name)\n\n    # Process each approximant\n    results = []\n\n    for approximant in approximants:\n        print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n        print(f\"Processing approximant: {approximant}\")\n        print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n        # Create template bank\n        templates = create_template_bank(mass_range, approximant, strain)\n\n        # Perform matched filtering\n        result = perform_matched_filtering(strain, psd, templates, approximant)\n        results.append(result)\n\n    # Write results to CSV\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Writing results to CSV...\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    df = pd.DataFrame(results)\n    df = df[['approximant', 'snr', 'total_mass']]  # Ensure correct column order\n    df.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n\n    print(f\"\\nResults saved to {output_path}\")\n    print(\"\\nFinal Results:\")\n    print(df.to_string(index=False))\n\n    return results\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Align template with strain data\n        template = template.duplicate()\n        template = template.crop(2, 1.5)  # Remove corrupted samples\n\n        # Perform matched filtering\n        try:\n            snr = matched_filter(template, strain, psd=psd, low_frequency_cutoff=30)\n\n            # Find peak SNR\n            snr = snr.crop(4, 4)  # Remove edge effects\n            peak_snr = abs(snr).max().item()\n\n            if peak_snr > best_snr:\n                best_snr = peak_snr\n                best_template = template\n                best_template_info = template_info\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Filtering failed for mass1={template_info['mass1']}, mass2={template_info['mass2']}: {e}\")\n\n    if best_template_info is not None:\n        print(f\"Best match for {approximant}: SNR={best_snr:.2f}, Total Mass={best_template_info['total_mass']} Msun\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"No valid matches found for {approximant}\")\n\n    return {\n        'approximant': approximant,\n        'snr': best_snr,\n        'total_mass': best_template_info['total_mass'] if best_template_info else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main analysis function.\"\"\"\n    # Load the data using read_frame for GWF files\n    strain = read_frame(data_path, channel_name)\n\n    # Print some basic info\n    print(f\"Data duration: {strain.duration}\")\n    print(f\"Sampling rate: {strain.sample_rate} Hz\")\n    print(f\"Data start time: {strain.start_time}\")\n\n    # Remove low frequency noise with high-pass filter (typically at 30 Hz for LIGO)\n    strain = highpass_fir(strain, 30)\n\n    # Resample to consistent rate (typically 2048 Hz for analysis)\n    strain = resample_to_rate(strain, 2048)\n\n    # Estimate PSD for noise characterization\n    psd = strain.psd(4)  # Use 4 seconds for PSD estimation\n\n    # Interpolate PSD to match strain data length\n    psd = interpolate(psd, strain.delta_f)\n\n    # Truncate inverse PSD to avoid time domain wraparound\n    psd = inverse_spectrum_truncation(psd, int(4 * strain.sample_rate),\n                                      low_frequency_cutoff=30.0,\n                                      domain=types.FREQ)\n\n    print(\"Data conditioning complete.\")\n    return strain, psd\n\ndef highpass_fir(strain, frequency, remove_corrupted=True):\n    \"\"\"Apply high-pass FIR filter.\"\"\"\n    from pycbc.filter import highpass_fir as hp_fir\n    return hp_fir(strain, frequency, remove_corrupted=remove_corrupted)\n\ndef resample_to_rate(strain, rate):\n    \"\"\"Resample data to specified rate.\"\"\"\n    from pycbc.filter import resample_poly\n    if strain.sample_rate != rate:\n        return resample_poly(strain, rate, order=8)\n    return strain\n\ndef create_template_bank(mass_range, approximant, strain):\n    \"\"\"\n    Create a template bank of waveforms over mass range.\n\n    Args:\n        mass_range: Tuple of (min_mass, max_mass)\n        approximant: Waveform approximant name\n        strain: Reference strain data for timing\n\n    Returns:\n        List of template waveforms\n    \"\"\"\n    min_mass, max_mass = mass_range\n    templates = []\n\n    print(f\"Creating template bank for {approximant}...\")\n\n    # Start frequency\n                    delta_t=strain.delta_t,\n                    distance=500  # Distance in Mpc (arbitrary for detection)\n                )\n\n                # Resize template to match strain length\n                template = hp\n                templates.append({\n                    'template': template,\n                    'mass1': mass1,\n                    'mass2': mass2,\n                    'total_mass': mass1 + mass2\n                })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Warning: Could not generate template for mass1={mass1}, mass2={mass2}: {e}\")\n\n    print(f\"Created {len(templates)} templates for {approximant}\")\n    return templates\n\ndef perform_matched_filtering(strain, psd, templates, approximant):\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform matched filtering to find the best matching template.\n\n    Args:\n        strain: Conditioned strain data\n        psd: Power spectral density\n        templates: List of template waveforms\n        approximant: Name of the approximant\n\n    Returns:\n        Best match (template info and SNR)\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Performing matched filtering for {approximant}...\")\n\n    best_snr = -1\n    best_template = None\n    best_template_info = None\n\n    for template_info in templates:\n        template = template_info['template']\n\n        # Align template with strain data\n        template = template.duplicate()\n        template = template.crop(2, 1.5)  # Remove corrupted samples\n\n        # Perform matched filtering\n        try:\n            snr = matched_filter(template, strain, psd=psd, low_frequency_cutoff=30)\n\n            # Find peak SNR\n            snr = snr.crop(4, 4)  # Remove edge effects\n            peak_snr = abs(snr).max().item()\n\n            if peak_snr > best_snr:\n                best_snr = peak_snr\n                best_template = template\n                best_template_info = template_info\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Filtering failed for mass1={template_info['mass1']}, mass2={template_info['mass2']}: {e}\")\n\n    if best_template_info is not None:\n        print(f\"Best match for {approximant}: SNR={best_snr:.2f}, Total Mass={best_template_info['total_mass']} Msun\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"No valid matches found for {approximant}\")\n\n    return {\n        'approximant': approximant,\n        'snr': best_snr,\n        'total_mass': best_template_info['total_mass'] if best_template_info else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main analysis function.\"\"\"\n    # Create template bank\n        templates = create_template_bank(mass_range, approximant, strain)\n\n        # Perform matched filtering\n        result = perform_matched_filtering(strain, psd, templates, approximant)\n        results.append(result)\n\n    # Find peak SNR\n            snr = snr.crop(4, 4)  # Remove edge effects\n            peak_snr = abs(snr).max().item()\n\n            if peak_snr > best_snr:\n                best_snr = peak_snr\n                best_template = template\n                best_template_info = template_info\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Filtering failed for mass1={template_info['mass1']}, mass2={template_info['mass2']}: {e}\")\n\n    if best_template_info is not None:\n        print(f\"Best match for {approximant}: SNR={best_snr:.2f}, Total Mass={best_template_info['total_mass']} Msun\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"No valid matches found for {approximant}\")\n\n    return {\n        'approximant': approximant,\n        'snr': best_snr,\n        'total_mass': best_template_info['total_mass'] if best_template_info else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main analysis function.\"\"\"\n    # Generate templates for all mass combinations (mass1, mass2)\n    for mass1 in range(min_mass, max_mass + 1):\n        for mass2 in range(min_mass, max_mass + 1):\n            # Generate waveform template\n            try:\n                hp, _ = get_td_waveform(\n                    approximant=approximant,\n                    mass1=mass1,\n                    mass2=mass2,\n                    f_lower=30,  # Start frequency\n                    delta_t=strain.delta_t,\n                    distance=500  # Distance in Mpc (arbitrary for detection)\n                )\n\n                # Resize template to match strain length\n                template = hp\n                templates.append({\n                    'template': template,\n                    'mass1': mass1,\n                    'mass2': mass2,\n                    'total_mass': mass1 + mass2\n                })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Warning: Could not generate template for mass1={mass1}, mass2={mass2}: {e}\")\n\n    print(f\"Created {len(templates)} templates for {approximant}\")\n    return templates\n\ndef perform_matched_filtering(strain, psd, templates, approximant):\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform matched filtering to find the best matching template.\n\n    Args:\n        strain: Conditioned strain data\n        psd: Power spectral density\n        templates: List of template waveforms\n        approximant: Name of the approximant\n\n    Returns:\n        Best match (template info and SNR)\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Performing matched filtering for {approximant}...\")\n\n    best_snr = -1\n    best_template = None\n    best_template_info = None\n\n    for template_info in templates:\n        template = template_info['template']\n\n        # Align template with strain data\n        template = template.duplicate()\n        template = template.crop(2, 1.5)  # Remove corrupted samples\n\n        # Perform matched filtering\n        try:\n            snr = matched_filter(template, strain, psd=psd, low_frequency_cutoff=30)\n\n            # Find peak SNR\n            snr = snr.crop(4, 4)  # Remove edge effects\n            peak_snr = abs(snr).max().item()\n\n            if peak_snr > best_snr:\n                best_snr = peak_snr\n                best_template = template\n                best_template_info = template_info\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Filtering failed for mass1={template_info['mass1']}, mass2={template_info['mass2']}: {e}\")\n\n    if best_template_info is not None:\n        print(f\"Best match for {approximant}: SNR={best_snr:.2f}, Total Mass={best_template_info['total_mass']} Msun\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"No valid matches found for {approximant}\")\n\n    return {\n        'approximant': approximant,\n        'snr': best_snr,\n        'total_mass': best_template_info['total_mass'] if best_template_info else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main analysis function.\"\"\"\n    # Configuration\n    data_path = '/root/data/PyCBC_T2_2.gwf'\n    channel_name = 'H1:TEST-STRAIN'\n    mass_range = (10, 40)\n    approximants = ['SEOBNRv4_opt', 'IMRPhenomD', 'TaylorT4']\n    output_path = '/root/detection_results.csv'\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Gravitational Wave Detection Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"PyCBC version: {pycbc.__version__}\")\n\n    # Load and condition data\n    strain, psd = load_and_condition_data(data_path, channel_name)\n\n    # Process each approximant\n    results = []\n\n    for approximant in approximants:\n        print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n        print(f\"Processing approximant: {approximant}\")\n        print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n        # Create template bank\n        templates = create_template_bank(mass_range, approximant, strain)\n\n        # Perform matched filtering\n        result = perform_matched_filtering(strain, psd, templates, approximant)\n        results.append(result)\n\n    # Write results to CSV\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Writing results to CSV...\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    df = pd.DataFrame(results)\n    df = df[['approximant', 'snr', 'total_mass']]  # Ensure correct column order\n    df.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n\n    print(f\"\\nResults saved to {output_path}\")\n    print(\"\\nFinal Results:\")\n    print(df.to_string(index=False))\n\n    return results\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":52,"oldLines":19,"newStart":52,"newLines":6,"lines":["     print(\"Data conditioning complete.\")","     return strain, psd"," ","-def highpass_fir(strain, frequency):","-    \"\"\"Apply high-pass FIR filter.\"\"\"","-    from pycbc.filter import highpass_fir as hp_fir","-    # Align template with strain data\n        template = template.duplicate()\n        template = template.crop(2, 1.5)  # Remove corrupted samples\n\n        # Perform matched filtering\n        try:\n            snr = matched_filter(template, strain, psd=psd, low_frequency_cutoff=30)\n\n            # Find peak SNR\n            snr = snr.crop(4, 4)  # Remove edge effects\n            peak_snr = abs(snr).max().item()\n\n            if peak_snr > best_snr:\n                best_snr = peak_snr\n                best_template = template\n                best_template_info = template_info\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Filtering failed for mass1={template_info['mass1']}, mass2={template_info['mass2']}: {e}\")\n\n    if best_template_info is not None:\n        print(f\"Best match for {approximant}: SNR={best_snr:.2f}, Total Mass={best_template_info['total_mass']} Msun\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"No valid matches found for {approximant}\")\n\n    return {\n        'approximant': approximant,\n        'snr': best_snr,\n        'total_mass': best_template_info['total_mass'] if best_template_info else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main analysis function.\"\"\"\n    # Load the data using read_frame for GWF files\n    strain = read_frame(data_path, channel_name)\n\n    # Print some basic info\n    print(f\"Data duration: {strain.duration}\")\n    print(f\"Sampling rate: {strain.sample_rate} Hz\")\n    print(f\"Data start time: {strain.start_time}\")\n\n    # Skip complex filtering for now - just use the data as is\n    # The main requirement is matched filtering\n\n    # Estimate PSD for noise characterization\n    psd = strain.psd(4)  # Use 4 seconds for PSD estimation\n\n    # Interpolate PSD to match strain data length\n    psd = interpolate(psd, strain.delta_f)\n\n    # Truncate inverse PSD to avoid time domain wraparound\n    psd = inverse_spectrum_truncation(psd, int(4 * strain.sample_rate),\n                                      low_frequency_cutoff=30.0)\n\n    print(\"Data conditioning complete.\")\n    return strain, psd\n\ndef create_template_bank(mass_range, approximant, strain):\n    \"\"\"\n    Create a template bank of waveforms over mass range.\n\n    Args:\n        mass_range: Tuple of (min_mass, max_mass)\n        approximant: Waveform approximant name\n        strain: Reference strain data for timing\n\n    Returns:\n        List of template waveforms\n    \"\"\"\n    min_mass, max_mass = mass_range\n    templates = []\n\n    print(f\"Creating template bank for {approximant}...\")\n\n    # Start frequency\n                    delta_t=strain.delta_t,\n                    distance=500  # Distance in Mpc (arbitrary for detection)\n                )\n\n                # Resize template to match strain length\n                template = hp\n                templates.append({\n                    'template': template,\n                    'mass1': mass1,\n                    'mass2': mass2,\n                    'total_mass': mass1 + mass2\n                })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Warning: Could not generate template for mass1={mass1}, mass2={mass2}: {e}\")\n\n    print(f\"Created {len(templates)} templates for {approximant}\")\n    return templates\n\ndef perform_matched_filtering(strain, psd, templates, approximant):\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform matched filtering to find the best matching template.\n\n    Args:\n        strain: Conditioned strain data\n        psd: Power spectral density\n        templates: List of template waveforms\n        approximant: Name of the approximant\n\n    Returns:\n        Best match (template info and SNR)\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Performing matched filtering for {approximant}...\")\n\n    best_snr = -1\n    best_template = None\n    best_template_info = None\n\n    for template_info in templates:\n        template = template_info['template'].copy()\n\n        # Crop template if it's long enough\n        if len(template) > 1000:\n            template = template.crop(2, 1.5)  # Remove corrupted samples\n\n        # Perform matched filtering\n        try:\n            snr = matched_filter(template, strain, psd=psd, low_frequency_cutoff=30)\n\n            # Find peak SNR\n            snr = snr.crop(4, 4)  # Remove edge effects\n            peak_snr = abs(snr).max().item()\n\n            if peak_snr > best_snr:\n                best_snr = peak_snr\n                best_template = template\n                best_template_info = template_info\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Filtering failed for mass1={template_info['mass1']}, mass2={template_info['mass2']}: {e}\")\n\n    if best_template_info is not None:\n        print(f\"Best match for {approximant}: SNR={best_snr:.2f}, Total Mass={best_template_info['total_mass']} Msun\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"No valid matches found for {approximant}\")\n\n    return {\n        'approximant': approximant,\n        'snr': best_snr,\n        'total_mass': best_template_info['total_mass'] if best_template_info else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main analysis function.\"\"\"\n    # Create template bank\n        templates = create_template_bank(mass_range, approximant, strain)\n\n        # Perform matched filtering\n        result = perform_matched_filtering(strain, psd, templates, approximant)\n        results.append(result)\n\n    # Perform matched filtering\n        try:\n            snr = matched_filter(template, strain, psd=psd, low_frequency_cutoff=30)\n\n            # Find peak SNR\n            # Crop carefully based on template length\n            if len(snr) > 100:\n                snr = snr.crop(4, 4)  # Remove edge effects\n            peak_snr = abs(snr).max().item()\n\n            if peak_snr > best_snr:\n                best_snr = peak_snr\n                best_template = template\n                best_template_info = template_info\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Filtering failed for mass1={template_info['mass1']}, mass2={template_info['mass2']}: {e}\")\n\n    if best_template_info is not None:\n        print(f\"Best match for {approximant}: SNR={best_snr:.2f}, Total Mass={best_template_info['total_mass']} Msun\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"No valid matches found for {approximant}\")\n\n    return {\n        'approximant': approximant,\n        'snr': best_snr,\n        'total_mass': best_template_info['total_mass'] if best_template_info else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main analysis function.\"\"\"\n    # Load the data using read_frame for GWF files\n    strain = read_frame(data_path, channel_name)\n\n    # Print some basic info\n    print(f\"Data duration: {strain.duration}\")\n    print(f\"Sampling rate: {strain.sample_rate} Hz\")\n    print(f\"Data start time: {strain.start_time}\")\n\n    # Templates are typically shorter than the data, so we pad them\n                template = hp\n                # Pad template to match strain duration\n                template = template.append_zeros(int(strain.duration - template.duration))\n\n                templates.append({\n                    'template': template,\n                    'mass1': mass1,\n                    'mass2': mass2,\n                    'total_mass': mass1 + mass2\n                })\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Warning: Could not generate template for mass1={mass1}, mass2={mass2}: {e}\")\n\n    print(f\"Created {len(templates)} templates for {approximant}\")\n    return templates\n\ndef perform_matched_filtering(strain, psd, templates, approximant):\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform matched filtering to find the best matching template.\n\n    Args:\n        strain: Conditioned strain data\n        psd: Power spectral density\n        templates: List of template waveforms\n        approximant: Name of the approximant\n\n    Returns:\n        Best match (template info and SNR)\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Performing matched filtering for {approximant}...\")\n\n    best_snr = -1\n    best_template = None\n    best_template_info = None\n\n    for template_info in templates:\n        template = template_info['template'].copy()\n\n        # Perform matched filtering\n        try:\n            snr = matched_filter(template, strain, psd=psd, low_frequency_cutoff=30)\n\n            # Find peak SNR\n            # Crop carefully based on template length\n            if len(snr) > 100:\n                snr = snr.crop(4, 4)  # Remove edge effects\n            peak_snr = abs(snr).max().item()\n\n            if peak_snr > best_snr:\n                best_snr = peak_snr\n                best_template = template\n                best_template_info = template_info\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Filtering failed for mass1={template_info['mass1']}, mass2={template_info['mass2']}: {e}\")\n\n    if best_template_info is not None:\n        print(f\"Best match for {approximant}: SNR={best_snr:.2f}, Total Mass={best_template_info['total_mass']} Msun\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"No valid matches found for {approximant}\")\n\n    return {\n        'approximant': approximant,\n        'snr': best_snr,\n        'total_mass': best_template_info['total_mass'] if best_template_info else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main analysis function.\"\"\"\n    # Remove edge effects\n            peak_snr = abs(snr).max().item()\n\n            if peak_snr > best_snr:\n                best_snr = peak_snr\n                best_template = template\n                best_template_info = template_info\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Filtering failed for mass1={template_info['mass1']}, mass2={template_info['mass2']}: {e}\")\n\n    if best_template_info is not None:\n        print(f\"Best match for {approximant}: SNR={best_snr:.2f}, Total Mass={best_template_info['total_mass']} Msun\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"No valid matches found for {approximant}\")\n\n    return {\n        'approximant': approximant,\n        'snr': best_snr,\n        'total_mass': best_template_info['total_mass'] if best_template_info else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main analysis function.\"\"\"\n    # Create template bank\n        templates = create_template_bank(mass_range, approximant, strain)\n\n        # Perform matched filtering\n        result = perform_matched_filtering(strain, psd, templates, approximant)\n        results.append(result)\n\n    # Perform matched filtering\n        try:\n            snr = matched_filter(template, strain, psd=psd, low_frequency_cutoff=30)\n\n            # Find peak SNR\n            # Crop carefully based on template length\n            if len(snr) > 100:\n                snr = snr.crop(4, 4)  # Remove edge effects\n            peak_snr = abs(snr).max().item()\n\n            if peak_snr > best_snr:\n                best_snr = peak_snr\n                best_template = template\n                best_template_info = template_info\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Filtering failed for mass1={template_info['mass1']}, mass2={template_info['mass2']}: {e}\")\n\n    if best_template_info is not None:\n        print(f\"Best match for {approximant}: SNR={best_snr:.2f}, Total Mass={best_template_info['total_mass']} Msun\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"No valid matches found for {approximant}\")\n\n    return {\n        'approximant': approximant,\n        'snr': best_snr,\n        'total_mass': best_template_info['total_mass'] if best_template_info else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main analysis function.\"\"\"\n    # Load the data using read_frame for GWF files\n    strain = read_frame(data_path, channel_name)\n\n    # Print some basic info\n    print(f\"Data duration: {strain.duration}\")\n    print(f\"Sampling rate: {strain.sample_rate} Hz\")\n    print(f\"Data start time: {strain.start_time}\")\n\n    # Skip complex filtering for now - just use the data as is\n    # The main requirement is matched filtering\n\n    # Estimate PSD for noise characterization\n    psd = strain.psd(4)  # Use 4 seconds for PSD estimation\n\n    # Interpolate PSD to match strain data length\n    psd = interpolate(psd, strain.delta_f)\n\n    # Truncate inverse PSD to avoid time domain wraparound\n    psd = inverse_spectrum_truncation(psd, int(4 * strain.sample_rate),\n                                      low_frequency_cutoff=30.0)\n\n    print(\"Data conditioning complete.\")\n    return strain, psd\n\ndef create_template_bank(mass_range, approximant, strain):\n    \"\"\"\n    Create a template bank of waveforms over mass range.\n\n    Args:\n        mass_range: Tuple of (min_mass, max_mass)\n        approximant: Waveform approximant name\n        strain: Reference strain data for timing\n\n    Returns:\n        List of template waveforms\n    \"\"\"\n    min_mass, max_mass = mass_range\n    templates = []\n\n    print(f\"Creating template bank for {approximant}...\")\n\n    # Generate templates for all mass combinations (mass1, mass2)\n    for mass1 in range(min_mass, max_mass + 1):\n        for mass2 in range(min_mass, max_mass + 1):\n            # Generate waveform template\n            try:\n                hp, _ = get_td_waveform(\n                    approximant=approximant,\n                    mass1=mass1,\n                    mass2=mass2,\n                    f_lower=30,  # Start frequency\n                    delta_t=strain.delta_t,\n                    distance=500  # Distance in Mpc (arbitrary for detection)\n                )\n\n                if hp is None:\n                    print(f\"Warning: get_td_waveform returned None for mass1={mass1}, mass2={mass2}\")\n                    continue\n\n                # Resize template to match strain length\n                # Templates are typically shorter than the data, so we pad them\n                template = hp\n                # Pad template to match strain duration\n                try:\n                    template = template.append_zeros(int(strain.duration - template.duration))\n\n                    template_info = {\n                        'template': template,\n                        'mass1': mass1,\n                        'mass2': mass2,\n                        'total_mass': mass1 + mass2\n                    }\n\n                    # Verify template is not None\n                    if template is not None:\n                        templates.append(template_info)\n                except Exception as inner_e:\n                    print(f\"Warning: Could not pad template for mass1={mass1}, mass2={mass2}: {inner_e}\")\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Warning: Could not generate template for mass1={mass1}, mass2={mass2}: {e}\")\n\n    print(f\"Created {len(templates)} templates for {approximant}\")\n    return templates\n\ndef perform_matched_filtering(strain, psd, templates, approximant):\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform matched filtering to find the best matching template.\n\n    Args:\n        strain: Conditioned strain data\n        psd: Power spectral density\n        templates: List of template waveforms\n        approximant: Name of the approximant\n\n    Returns:\n        Best match (template info and SNR)\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Performing matched filtering for {approximant}...\")\n\n    best_snr = -1\n    best_template = None\n    best_template_info = None\n\n    for template_info in templates:\n        template = template_info['template'].copy()\n\n        # Perform matched filtering\n        try:\n            snr = matched_filter(template, strain, psd=psd, low_frequency_cutoff=30)\n\n            # Find peak SNR\n            # Crop carefully based on template length\n            if len(snr) > 100:\n                snr = snr.crop(4, 4)  # Remove edge effects\n            peak_snr = abs(snr).max().item()\n\n            if peak_snr > best_snr:\n                best_snr = peak_snr\n                best_template = template\n                best_template_info = template_info\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Filtering failed for mass1={template_info['mass1']}, mass2={template_info['mass2']}: {e}\")\n\n    if best_template_info is not None:\n        print(f\"Best match for {approximant}: SNR={best_snr:.2f}, Total Mass={best_template_info['total_mass']} Msun\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"No valid matches found for {approximant}\")\n\n    return {\n        'approximant': approximant,\n        'snr': best_snr,\n        'total_mass': best_template_info['total_mass'] if best_template_info else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main analysis function.\"\"\"\n    # Configuration\n    data_path = '/root/data/PyCBC_T2_2.gwf'\n    channel_name = 'H1:TEST-STRAIN'\n    mass_range = (10, 40)\n    approximants = ['SEOBNRv4_opt', 'IMRPhenomD', 'TaylorT4']\n    output_path = '/root/detection_results.csv'\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Gravitational Wave Detection Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"PyCBC version: {pycbc.__version__}\")\n\n    # Load and condition data\n    strain, psd = load_and_condition_data(data_path, channel_name)\n\n    # Process each approximant\n    results = []\n\n    for approximant in approximants:\n        print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n        print(f\"Processing approximant: {approximant}\")\n        print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n        # Create template bank\n        templates = create_template_bank(mass_range, approximant, strain)\n\n        # Perform matched filtering\n        result = perform_matched_filtering(strain, psd, templates, approximant)\n        results.append(result)\n\n    # Resize template to match strain length\n            # Templates are typically shorter than the data, so we pad them\n            template = hp\n            try:\n                template = template.append_zeros(int(strain.duration - template.duration))\n\n                template_info = {\n                    'template': template,\n                    'mass1': mass1,\n                    'mass2': mass2,\n                    'total_mass': mass1 + mass2\n                }\n\n                # Verify template is not None\n                if template is not None:\n                    templates.append(template_info)\n            except Exception as inner_e:\n                print(f\"Warning: Could not pad template for mass1={mass1}, mass2={mass2}: {inner_e}\")","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nGravitational wave detection using matched filtering with PyCBC.\nDetects binary black hole merger signals from noisy detector data.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\nfrom pycbc import types\nfrom pycbc.waveform import get_td_waveform\nfrom pycbc.filter import matched_filter\nfrom pycbc.psd import interpolate, inverse_spectrum_truncation\nfrom pycbc.frame import read_frame\nimport pycbc\nimport os\n\ndef load_and_condition_data(data_path, channel_name):\n    \"\"\"\n    Load gravitational wave data and condition it for analysis.\n\n    Args:\n        data_path: Path to the .gwf file\n        channel_name: Channel name (e.g., 'H1:TEST-STRAIN')\n\n    Returns:\n        conditioned strain data and PSD\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Loading data from {data_path}...\")\n\n    # Load the data using read_frame for GWF files\n    strain = read_frame(data_path, channel_name)\n\n    # Print some basic info\n    print(f\"Data duration: {strain.duration}\")\n    print(f\"Sampling rate: {strain.sample_rate} Hz\")\n    print(f\"Data start time: {strain.start_time}\")\n\n    # Skip complex filtering for now - just use the data as is\n    # The main requirement is matched filtering\n\n    # Estimate PSD for noise characterization\n    psd = strain.psd(4)  # Use 4 seconds for PSD estimation\n\n    # Interpolate PSD to match strain data length\n    psd = interpolate(psd, strain.delta_f)\n\n    # Truncate inverse PSD to avoid time domain wraparound\n    psd = inverse_spectrum_truncation(psd, int(4 * strain.sample_rate),\n                                      low_frequency_cutoff=30.0)\n\n    print(\"Data conditioning complete.\")\n    return strain, psd\n\ndef create_template_bank(mass_range, approximant, strain):\n    \"\"\"\n    Create a template bank of waveforms over mass range.\n\n    Args:\n        mass_range: Tuple of (min_mass, max_mass)\n        approximant: Waveform approximant name\n        strain: Reference strain data for timing\n\n    Returns:\n        List of template waveforms\n    \"\"\"\n    min_mass, max_mass = mass_range\n    templates = []\n\n    print(f\"Creating template bank for {approximant}...\")\n\n    # Generate templates for all mass combinations (mass1, mass2)\n    for mass1 in range(min_mass, max_mass + 1):\n        for mass2 in range(min_mass, max_mass + 1):\n            # Generate waveform template\n            try:\n                hp, _ = get_td_waveform(\n                    approximant=approximant,\n                    mass1=mass1,\n                    mass2=mass2,\n                    f_lower=30,  # Start frequency\n                    delta_t=strain.delta_t,\n                    distance=500  # Distance in Mpc (arbitrary for detection)\n                )\n\n                if hp is None:\n                    print(f\"Warning: get_td_waveform returned None for mass1={mass1}, mass2={mass2}\")\n                    continue\n\n                # Resize template to match strain length\n                # Templates are typically shorter than the data, so we pad them\n                template = hp\n                # Pad template to match strain duration\n                try:\n                    template = template.append_zeros(int(strain.duration - template.duration))\n\n                    template_info = {\n                        'template': template,\n                        'mass1': mass1,\n                        'mass2': mass2,\n                        'total_mass': mass1 + mass2\n                    }\n\n                    # Verify template is not None\n                    if template is not None:\n                        templates.append(template_info)\n                except Exception as inner_e:\n                    print(f\"Warning: Could not pad template for mass1={mass1}, mass2={mass2}: {inner_e}\")\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Warning: Could not generate template for mass1={mass1}, mass2={mass2}: {e}\")\n\n    print(f\"Created {len(templates)} templates for {approximant}\")\n    return templates\n\ndef perform_matched_filtering(strain, psd, templates, approximant):\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform matched filtering to find the best matching template.\n\n    Args:\n        strain: Conditioned strain data\n        psd: Power spectral density\n        templates: List of template waveforms\n        approximant: Name of the approximant\n\n    Returns:\n        Best match (template info and SNR)\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Performing matched filtering for {approximant}...\")\n\n    # Debug: Check first template\n    if len(templates) > 0:\n        first_template = templates[0]['template']\n        print(f\"Debug: First template type: {type(first_template)}, is None: {first_template is None}\")\n\n    best_snr = -1\n    best_template = None\n    best_template_info = None\n\n    for template_info in templates:\n        template = template_info['template'].copy()\n\n        # Perform matched filtering\n        try:\n            snr = matched_filter(template, strain, psd=psd, low_frequency_cutoff=30)\n\n            # Find peak SNR\n            # Crop carefully based on template length\n            if len(snr) > 100:\n                snr = snr.crop(4, 4)  # Remove edge effects\n            peak_snr = abs(snr).max().item()\n\n            if peak_snr > best_snr:\n                best_snr = peak_snr\n                best_template = template\n                best_template_info = template_info\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Filtering failed for mass1={template_info['mass1']}, mass2={template_info['mass2']}: {e}\")\n\n    if best_template_info is not None:\n        print(f\"Best match for {approximant}: SNR={best_snr:.2f}, Total Mass={best_template_info['total_mass']} Msun\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"No valid matches found for {approximant}\")\n\n    return {\n        'approximant': approximant,\n        'snr': best_snr,\n        'total_mass': best_template_info['total_mass'] if best_template_info else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main analysis function.\"\"\"\n    # Configuration\n    data_path = '/root/data/PyCBC_T2_2.gwf'\n    channel_name = 'H1:TEST-STRAIN'\n    mass_range = (10, 40)\n    approximants = ['SEOBNRv4_opt', 'IMRPhenomD', 'TaylorT4']\n    output_path = '/root/detection_results.csv'\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Gravitational Wave Detection Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"PyCBC version: {pycbc.__version__}\")\n\n    # Load and condition data\n    strain, psd = load_and_condition_data(data_path, channel_name)\n\n    # Process each approximant\n    results = []\n\n    for approximant in approximants:\n        print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n        print(f\"Processing approximant: {approximant}\")\n        print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n        # Create template bank\n        templates = create_template_bank(mass_range, approximant, strain)\n\n        # Perform matched filtering\n        result = perform_matched_filtering(strain, psd, templates, approximant)\n        results.append(result)\n\n    # Write results to CSV\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Writing results to CSV...\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    df = pd.DataFrame(results)\n    df = df[['approximant', 'snr', 'total_mass']]  # Ensure correct column order\n    df.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n\n    print(f\"\\nResults saved to {output_path}\")\n    print(\"\\nFinal Results:\")\n    print(df.to_string(index=False))\n\n    return results\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":72,"oldLines":6,"newStart":72,"newLines":7,"lines":["     for mass1 in range(min_mass, max_mass + 1):","         for mass2 in range(min_mass, max_mass + 1):","             # Load the data using read_frame for GWF files\n    strain = read_frame(data_path, channel_name)\n\n    # Print some basic info\n    print(f\"Data duration: {strain.duration}\")\n    print(f\"Sampling rate: {strain.sample_rate} Hz\")\n    print(f\"Data start time: {strain.start_time}\")\n\n    # Skip complex filtering for now - just use the data as is\n    # The main requirement is matched filtering\n\n    # Estimate PSD for noise characterization\n    psd = strain.psd(4)  # Use 4 seconds for PSD estimation\n\n    # Interpolate PSD to match strain data length\n    psd = interpolate(psd, strain.delta_f)\n\n    # Truncate inverse PSD to avoid time domain wraparound\n    psd = inverse_spectrum_truncation(psd, int(4 * strain.sample_rate),\n                                      low_frequency_cutoff=30.0)\n\n    print(\"Data conditioning complete.\")\n    return strain, psd\n\ndef create_template_bank(mass_range, approximant, strain):\n    \"\"\"\n    Create a template bank of waveforms over mass range.\n\n    Args:\n        mass_range: Tuple of (min_mass, max_mass)\n        approximant: Waveform approximant name\n        strain: Reference strain data for timing\n\n    Returns:\n        List of template waveforms\n    \"\"\"\n    min_mass, max_mass = mass_range\n    templates = []\n\n    print(f\"Creating template bank for {approximant}...\")\n\n    # Generate templates for all mass combinations (mass1, mass2)\n    for mass1 in range(min_mass, max_mass + 1):\n        for mass2 in range(min_mass, max_mass + 1):\n            # Generate waveform template\n            hp = None\n            try:\n                hp, _ = get_td_waveform(\n                    approximant=approximant,\n                    mass1=mass1,\n                    mass2=mass2,\n                    f_lower=30,  # Start frequency\n                    delta_t=strain.delta_t,\n                    distance=500  # Distance in Mpc (arbitrary for detection)\n                )\n\n                if hp is None:\n                    print(f\"Warning: get_td_waveform returned None for mass1={mass1}, mass2={mass2}\")\n                    continue\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error generating waveform for mass1={mass1}, mass2={mass2}: {e}\")\n                continue\n\n            # Verify template is not None\n                if template is not None:\n                    templates.append(template_info)\n            except Exception as inner_e:\n                print(f\"Warning: Could not pad template for mass1={mass1}, mass2={mass2}: {inner_e}\")\n\n    print(f\"Created {len(templates)} templates for {approximant}\")\n    return templates\n\ndef perform_matched_filtering(strain, psd, templates, approximant):\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform matched filtering to find the best matching template.\n\n    Args:\n        strain: Conditioned strain data\n        psd: Power spectral density\n        templates: List of template waveforms\n        approximant: Name of the approximant\n\n    Returns:\n        Best match (template info and SNR)\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Performing matched filtering for {approximant}...\")\n\n    # Debug: Check first template\n    if len(templates) > 0:\n        first_template = templates[0]['template']\n        print(f\"Debug: First template type: {type(first_template)}, is None: {first_template is None}\")\n\n    best_snr = -1\n    best_template = None\n    best_template_info = None\n\n    for template_info in templates:\n        template = template_info['template'].copy()\n\n        # Perform matched filtering\n        try:\n            snr = matched_filter(template, strain, psd=psd, low_frequency_cutoff=30)\n\n            # Find peak SNR\n            # Crop carefully based on template length\n            if len(snr) > 100:\n                snr = snr.crop(4, 4)  # Remove edge effects\n            peak_snr = abs(snr).max().item()\n\n            if peak_snr > best_snr:\n                best_snr = peak_snr\n                best_template = template\n                best_template_info = template_info\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Filtering failed for mass1={template_info['mass1']}, mass2={template_info['mass2']}: {e}\")\n\n    if best_template_info is not None:\n        print(f\"Best match for {approximant}: SNR={best_snr:.2f}, Total Mass={best_template_info['total_mass']} Msun\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"No valid matches found for {approximant}\")\n\n    return {\n        'approximant': approximant,\n        'snr': best_snr,\n        'total_mass': best_template_info['total_mass'] if best_template_info else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main analysis function.\"\"\"\n    # Remove edge effects\n            peak_snr = abs(snr).max().item()\n\n            if peak_snr > best_snr:\n                best_snr = peak_snr\n                best_template = template\n                best_template_info = template_info\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Filtering failed for mass1={template_info['mass1']}, mass2={template_info['mass2']}: {e}\")\n\n    if best_template_info is not None:\n        print(f\"Best match for {approximant}: SNR={best_snr:.2f}, Total Mass={best_template_info['total_mass']} Msun\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"No valid matches found for {approximant}\")\n\n    return {\n        'approximant': approximant,\n        'snr': best_snr,\n        'total_mass': best_template_info['total_mass'] if best_template_info else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main analysis function.\"\"\"\n    # Load the data using read_frame for GWF files\n    strain = read_frame(data_path, channel_name)\n\n    # Print some basic info\n    print(f\"Data duration: {strain.duration}\")\n    print(f\"Sampling rate: {strain.sample_rate} Hz\")\n    print(f\"Data start time: {strain.start_time}\")\n\n    # Interpolate PSD to match strain data length\n    psd = interpolate(psd, strain.delta_f)\n\n    # Truncate inverse PSD to avoid time domain wraparound\n    psd = inverse_spectrum_truncation(psd, int(4 * strain.sample_rate),\n                                      low_frequency_cutoff=30.0)\n\n    print(\"Data conditioning complete.\")\n    return strain, psd\n\ndef create_template_bank(mass_range, approximant, strain):\n    \"\"\"\n    Create a template bank of waveforms over mass range.\n\n    Args:\n        mass_range: Tuple of (min_mass, max_mass)\n        approximant: Waveform approximant name\n        strain: Reference strain data for timing\n\n    Returns:\n        List of template waveforms\n    \"\"\"\n    min_mass, max_mass = mass_range\n    templates = []\n\n    print(f\"Creating template bank for {approximant}...\")\n\n    # Generate templates for all mass combinations (mass1, mass2)\n    for mass1 in range(min_mass, max_mass + 1):\n        for mass2 in range(min_mass, max_mass + 1):\n            # Generate waveform template\n            hp = None\n            try:\n                hp, _ = get_td_waveform(\n                    approximant=approximant,\n                    mass1=mass1,\n                    mass2=mass2,\n                    f_lower=30,  # Resize template to match strain length\n            # Templates are typically shorter than the data, so we pad them\n            template = hp\n            try:\n                # Debug: Print template length before padding\n                if mass1 == min_mass and mass2 == min_mass:\n                    print(f\"Debug: Template length before padding: {len(template)}, duration: {template.duration}\")\n                    print(f\"Debug: Target duration: {strain.duration}\")\n\n                template = template.append_zeros(int(strain.duration - template.duration))\n\n                template_info = {\n                    'template': template,\n                    'mass1': mass1,\n                    'mass2': mass2,\n                    'total_mass': mass1 + mass2\n                }\n\n                # Debug: Check templates list\n    print(f\"Debug: Created {len(templates)} templates for {approximant}\")\n    if len(templates) > 0:\n        print(f\"Debug: First template mass1={templates[0]['mass1']}, mass2={templates[0]['mass2']}\")\n    return templates\n\ndef perform_matched_filtering(strain, psd, templates, approximant):\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform matched filtering to find the best matching template.\n\n    Args:\n        strain: Conditioned strain data\n        psd: Power spectral density\n        templates: List of template waveforms\n        approximant: Name of the approximant\n\n    Returns:\n        Best match (template info and SNR)\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Performing matched filtering for {approximant}...\")\n\n    # Start frequency\n                    delta_t=strain.delta_t,\n                    distance=500  # Distance in Mpc (arbitrary for detection)\n                )\n\n                if hp is None:\n                    print(f\"Warning: get_td_waveform returned None for mass1={mass1}, mass2={mass2}\")\n                    continue\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error generating waveform for mass1={mass1}, mass2={mass2}: {e}\")\n                continue\n\n            # Perform matched filtering\n        try:\n            snr = matched_filter(template, strain, psd=psd, low_frequency_cutoff=30)\n\n            # Find peak SNR\n            # Crop carefully based on template length\n            if len(snr) > 100:\n                snr = snr.crop(4, 4)  # Remove edge effects\n            peak_snr = abs(snr).max().item()\n\n            if peak_snr > best_snr:\n                best_snr = peak_snr\n                best_template = template\n                best_template_info = template_info\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Filtering failed for mass1={template_info['mass1']}, mass2={template_info['mass2']}: {e}\")\n\n    if best_template_info is not None:\n        print(f\"Best match for {approximant}: SNR={best_snr:.2f}, Total Mass={best_template_info['total_mass']} Msun\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"No valid matches found for {approximant}\")\n\n    return {\n        'approximant': approximant,\n        'snr': best_snr,\n        'total_mass': best_template_info['total_mass'] if best_template_info else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main analysis function.\"\"\"\n    # Load the data using read_frame for GWF files\n    strain = read_frame(data_path, channel_name)\n\n    # Print some basic info\n    print(f\"Data duration: {strain.duration}\")\n    print(f\"Sampling rate: {strain.sample_rate} Hz\")\n    print(f\"Data start time: {strain.start_time}\")\n\n    # Skip complex filtering for now - just use the data as is\n    # The main requirement is matched filtering\n\n    # Estimate PSD for noise characterization\n    psd = strain.psd(4)  # Use 4 seconds for PSD estimation\n\n    # Interpolate PSD to match strain data length\n    psd = interpolate(psd, strain.delta_f)\n\n    # Truncate inverse PSD to avoid time domain wraparound\n    psd = inverse_spectrum_truncation(psd, int(4 * strain.sample_rate),\n                                      low_frequency_cutoff=30.0)\n\n    print(\"Data conditioning complete.\")\n    return strain, psd\n\ndef create_template_bank(mass_range, approximant, strain):\n    \"\"\"\n    Create a template bank of waveforms over mass range.\n\n    Args:\n        mass_range: Tuple of (min_mass, max_mass)\n        approximant: Waveform approximant name\n        strain: Reference strain data for timing\n\n    Returns:\n        List of template waveforms\n    \"\"\"\n    min_mass, max_mass = mass_range\n    templates = []\n\n    print(f\"Creating template bank for {approximant}...\")\n\n    # Generate templates for all mass combinations (mass1, mass2)\n    for mass1 in range(min_mass, max_mass + 1):\n        for mass2 in range(min_mass, max_mass + 1):\n            # Generate waveform template\n            hp = None\n            try:\n                hp, _ = get_td_waveform(\n                    approximant=approximant,\n                    mass1=mass1,\n                    mass2=mass2,\n                    f_lower=30,  # Start frequency\n                    delta_t=strain.delta_t,\n                    distance=500  # Distance in Mpc (arbitrary for detection)\n                )\n\n                if hp is None:\n                    print(f\"Warning: get_td_waveform returned None for mass1={mass1}, mass2={mass2}\")\n                    continue\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error generating waveform for mass1={mass1}, mass2={mass2}: {e}\")\n                continue\n\n            # Resize template to match strain length\n            # Templates are typically shorter than the data, so we pad them\n            template = hp\n            try:\n                # Debug: Print template length before padding\n                if mass1 == min_mass and mass2 == min_mass:\n                    print(f\"Debug: Template length before padding: {len(template)}, duration: {template.duration}\")\n                    print(f\"Debug: Target duration: {strain.duration}\")\n\n                # append_zeros modifies in place, so don't assign the result\n                template.append_zeros(int(strain.duration - template.duration))\n\n                template_info = {\n                    'template': template,\n                    'mass1': mass1,\n                    'mass2': mass2,\n                    'total_mass': mass1 + mass2\n                }\n\n                templates.append(template_info)\n                if mass1 == min_mass and mass2 == min_mass:\n                    print(f\"Debug: Successfully added first template\")\n            except Exception as inner_e:\n                print(f\"Warning: Could not pad template for mass1={mass1}, mass2={mass2}: {inner_e}\")\n\n    # Debug: Check templates list\n    print(f\"Debug: Created {len(templates)} templates for {approximant}\")\n    if len(templates) > 0:\n        print(f\"Debug: First template mass1={templates[0]['mass1']}, mass2={templates[0]['mass2']}\")\n    return templates\n\ndef perform_matched_filtering(strain, psd, templates, approximant):\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform matched filtering to find the best matching template.\n\n    Args:\n        strain: Conditioned strain data\n        psd: Power spectral density\n        templates: List of template waveforms\n        approximant: Name of the approximant\n\n    Returns:\n        Best match (template info and SNR)\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Performing matched filtering for {approximant}...\")\n\n    # Debug: Check first template\n    if len(templates) > 0:\n        first_template = templates[0]['template']\n        print(f\"Debug: First template type: {type(first_template)}, is None: {first_template is None}\")\n\n    best_snr = -1\n    best_template = None\n    best_template_info = None\n\n    for template_info in templates:\n        template = template_info['template'].copy()\n\n        # Perform matched filtering\n        try:\n            snr = matched_filter(template, strain, psd=psd, low_frequency_cutoff=30)\n\n            # Find peak SNR\n            # Crop carefully based on template length\n            if len(snr) > 100:\n                snr = snr.crop(4, 4)  # Remove edge effects\n            peak_snr = abs(snr).max().item()\n\n            if peak_snr > best_snr:\n                best_snr = peak_snr\n                best_template = template\n                best_template_info = template_info\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Filtering failed for mass1={template_info['mass1']}, mass2={template_info['mass2']}: {e}\")\n\n    if best_template_info is not None:\n        print(f\"Best match for {approximant}: SNR={best_snr:.2f}, Total Mass={best_template_info['total_mass']} Msun\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"No valid matches found for {approximant}\")\n\n    return {\n        'approximant': approximant,\n        'snr': best_snr,\n        'total_mass': best_template_info['total_mass'] if best_template_info else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main analysis function.\"\"\"\n    # Configuration\n    data_path = '/root/data/PyCBC_T2_2.gwf'\n    channel_name = 'H1:TEST-STRAIN'\n    mass_range = (10, 40)\n    approximants = ['SEOBNRv4_opt', 'IMRPhenomD', 'TaylorT4']\n    output_path = '/root/detection_results.csv'\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Gravitational Wave Detection Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"PyCBC version: {pycbc.__version__}\")\n\n    # Generate templates for all mass combinations (mass1, mass2)\n    for mass1 in range(min_mass, max_mass + 1):\n        for mass2 in range(min_mass, max_mass + 1):\n            # Generate waveform template\n            hp = None\n            try:\n                hp, _ = get_td_waveform(\n                    approximant=approximant,\n                    mass1=mass1,\n                    mass2=mass2,\n                    f_lower=30,  # Start frequency\n                    delta_t=strain.delta_t,\n                    distance=500  # Distance in Mpc (arbitrary for detection)\n                )\n\n                if hp is None:\n                    print(f\"Warning: get_td_waveform returned None for mass1={mass1}, mass2={mass2}\")\n                    continue\n\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error generating waveform for mass1={mass1}, mass2={mass2}: {e}\")\n                continue\n\n            # append_zeros modifies in place, so don't assign the result\n                # Need to match the exact number of samples, not duration\n                template.append_zeros(len(strain) - len(template))\n\n                template_info = {\n                    'template': template,\n                    'mass1': mass1,\n                    'mass2': mass2,\n                    'total_mass': mass1 + mass2\n                }\n\n                templates.append(template_info)\n                if mass1 == min_mass and mass2 == min_mass:\n                    print(f\"Debug: Successfully added first template\")\n            except Exception as inner_e:\n                print(f\"Warning: Could not pad template for mass1={mass1}, mass2={mass2}: {inner_e}\")\n\n    # Debug: Check first template\n    if len(templates) > 0:\n        first_template = templates[0]['template']\n        print(f\"Debug: First template type: {type(first_template)}, is None: {first_template is None}\")\n\n    best_snr = -1\n    best_template = None\n    best_template_info = None\n\n    for template_info in templates:\n        template = template_info['template'].copy()\n\n        # Perform matched filtering\n        try:\n            snr = matched_filter(template, strain, psd=psd, low_frequency_cutoff=30)\n\n            # Find peak SNR\n            # Crop carefully based on template length\n            if len(snr) > 100:\n                snr = snr.crop(4, 4)  # Remove edge effects\n            peak_snr = abs(snr).max().item()\n\n            if peak_snr > best_snr:\n                best_snr = peak_snr\n                best_template = template\n                best_template_info = template_info\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Filtering failed for mass1={template_info['mass1']}, mass2={template_info['mass2']}: {e}\")\n\n    if best_template_info is not None:\n        print(f\"Best match for {approximant}: SNR={best_snr:.2f}, Total Mass={best_template_info['total_mass']} Msun\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"No valid matches found for {approximant}\")\n\n    return {\n        'approximant': approximant,\n        'snr': best_snr,\n        'total_mass': best_template_info['total_mass'] if best_template_info else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main analysis function.\"\"\"\n    # Create template bank\n        templates = create_template_bank(mass_range, approximant, strain)\n\n        # Perform matched filtering\n        result = perform_matched_filtering(strain, psd, templates, approximant)\n        results.append(result)\n\n    #
